The theorem about reducible Dehn surgeries on knots:
**Gordon-Luecke Theorem** (or Gordon's theorem): Let K be a nontrivial knot in S^3. If a Dehn surgery on K yields a reducible 3-manifold, then K is a cable knot and the surgery slope is the cabling slope. Moreover, if K is the (p,q)-cable of a knot C, then the surgery at the cabling slope pq yields \(L(p,q) \# S^3_{q/p}(C)\) (up to orientation and depending on conventions).
